    I can't render an MP4 directly, and I can't see your uploaded icon or screenshots in this chat. So I built the commercial as a single-file animated HTML piece: a 25-second, 9:16 timeline with neon particles, ribbons, a 3D-style phone and glass UI. You load your real icon into it, then record it to video.

    How to use it

    1. Save the code below as ji-insta-x-promo.html and open it in Chrome.
    2. Click Load app icon and choose your icon file. Without one, it uses a placeholder "JI x ♕" icon.
    3. Click Play.
    4. To export, record the page with OBS (Browser Source or Window Capture at 1080×1920) or any screen recorder. Add music in CapCut or Premiere.

    Timeline

    TimeScene
    0–4sDark screen, neon glow, icon emerges, title, "Customize Your Experience"
    4–8.5sPhone with settings. The 8 rows appear one by one and their switches animate.
    8.5–11.5sUI sweeps into a clean view, "Hide what you don't need.", then "Your Feed / Your Choice"
    11.5–17.5s6 feature shots, 1s each
    17.5–20.5sBrand reveal with neon ribbons
    20.5–25sCTA with floating phone, DOWNLOAD APK, Android 8.0+, logo, fade to black

<script>
/* Put your icon path/URL/data-URI here, or use the "Load app icon" button */
let ICON_SRC = "";

const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const stage=$('#stage'), cv=$('#cv'), ctx=cv.getContext('2d');
const FB='<div class="fb"><b>JI</b><i>x ♕</i></div>';
function applyIcon(){document.querySelectorAll('.appicon').forEach(e=>e.innerHTML=ICON_SRC?`<img src="${ICON_SRC}" alt="">`:FB)}
$('#file').onchange=e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(f){ICON_SRC=URL.createObjectURL(f);applyIcon()}};

/* settings rows */
const ROWS=['Home feed','Explore','Reels','Friends in Reels','Stories','Instants','Notes','Suggested accounts'];
$('#rows').innerHTML=ROWS.map((n,i)=>`<div class="row" style="--i:${i}"><i class="dot"></i><span class="l">${n}</span><div class="sw ${n==='Stories'?'back':''}" style="--i:${i}"></div></div>`).join('');

/* features */
const ic=p=>`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="url(#g)" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">${p}</svg>`;
const FEATS=[
 ['Hide Sections',ic('<path d="M3 3l18 18"/><path d="M10.6 6.1A9.8 9.8 0 0 1 12 6c5 0 8.5 4 9.5 6-.4.8-1.2 2-2.4 3.2M6.6 7.6C4.5 9 3.1 10.9 2.5 12c1 2 4.5 6 9.5 6 1.5 0 2.8-.4 4-.9"/><path d="M9.9 9.9a3 3 0 0 0 4.2 4.2"/>'),'#a855f7','#d946ef'],
 ['Privacy First',ic('<path d="M12 3l8 3v6c0 4.5-3.2 7.8-8 9-4.8-1.2-8-4.5-8-9V6z"/><path d="M8.5 12l2.4 2.4 4.6-4.8"/>'),'#d946ef','#ff2e93'],
 ['Easy Control',ic('<path d="M4 7h9M17 7h3M4 17h3M11 17h9"/><circle cx="15" cy="7" r="2"/><circle cx="9" cy="17" r="2"/>'),'#ff2e93','#ff8a1f'],
 ['Clear Cache',ic('<path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3M6 7l1 13h10l1-13M10 11v6M14 11v6"/>'),'#ff8a1f','#ffd23f'],
 ['Automatic Updates',ic('<path d="M19.5 10A8 8 0 0 0 5.6 7M4.5 14a8 8 0 0 0 13.9 3"/><path d="M20 4v6h-6M4 20v-6h6"/>'),'#7c3aed','#ff2e93'],
 ['Permanent Lock',ic('<rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="10" rx="2.5"/><path d="M8 11V8a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3"/><circle cx="12" cy="16" r="1.2"/>'),'#ff2e93','#ffd23f']
];
$('#s4').innerHTML=FEATS.map((f,i)=>`<div class="feat" style="--i:${i};--c1:${f[2]};--c2:${f[3]}">
  <div class="fglow"></div><div class="fn">0${i+1} / 06</div>
  <div class="fi"><div class="ring"></div>${f[1]}</div><div class="fl">${f[0]}</div></div>`).join('');

applyIcon();

/* ---------- background canvas ---------- */
const PAL=['#7c3aed','#a855f7','#d946ef','#ff2e93','#ff8a1f','#ffd23f'];
const parts=Array.from({length:110},()=>({x:Math.random(),y:Math.random(),z:Math.random(),c:PAL[Math.random()*6|0],s:.03+Math.random()*.07,p:Math.random()*6.28}));
let rib=0, ribT=0, dpr=1, last=0;
function fit(){
  stage.style.fontSize=stage.clientWidth/100+'px';
  dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);
  cv.width=stage.clientWidth*dpr; cv.height=stage.clientHeight*dpr;
}
addEventListener('resize',fit); fit();

function draw(t){
  const W=cv.width,H=cv.height,dt=Math.min(.05,t-last);last=t;
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';ctx.fillStyle='#000';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
  const k=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(t-1)/3));
  rib+=(ribT-rib)*.04;
  const amb=k*.28+rib*.25;
  [[.15,.2,0],[.9,.55,3],[.3,.95,1]].forEach(([x,y,c],i)=>{
    const cx=(x+.06*Math.sin(t*.4+i))*W,cy=(y+.04*Math.cos(t*.3+i))*H;
    const col=PAL[(c+Math.floor(t/6))%6];
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,0,cx,cy,W*.9);
    g.addColorStop(0,col+'88');g.addColorStop(1,col+'00');
    ctx.globalAlpha=amb;ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
  });
  // light-trail particles
  ctx.lineCap='round';
  parts.forEach(p=>{
    p.y-=p.s*dt*(.4+p.z);if(p.y<-.05)p.y=1.05;
    const a=k*(.25+.6*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*1.3+p.p)))*(.3+p.z*.7);
    ctx.globalAlpha=a;ctx.strokeStyle=p.c;ctx.lineWidth=(1+p.z*2.4)*dpr;
    const x=p.x*W,y=p.y*H;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x,y+(8+p.z*34)*dpr);ctx.stroke();
  });
  // neon ribbons
  if(rib>.01){
    ctx.lineWidth=1.3*dpr;
    const R=['#7c3aed','#d946ef','#ff2e93','#ff8a1f','#ffd23f','#a855f7'];
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
      ctx.strokeStyle=R[i];
      for(let j=0;j<16;j++){
        ctx.globalAlpha=rib*(.18+.25*(1-Math.abs(j-8)/8));
        ctx.beginPath();
        for(let s=0;s<=60;s++){
          const u=s/60,x=u*W;
          const y=H*(.36+.05*i)+Math.sin(u*5+t*.9+i*1.1+j*.07)*H*.07*(.5+u)+Math.cos(u*9-t*.7+i)*H*.012+j*1.5*dpr;
          s?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
  }
  ctx.globalAlpha=1;
}

/* ---------- timeline ---------- */
const SC=[['s1',0,4.4],['s2',4,11.6],['s3',8.5,11.6],['s4',11.5,17.6],['s5',17.5,20.6],['s6',20.5,25.5]];
const TOTAL=25.4;
let t0=0,running=false;
function frame(now){
  if(!running)return;
  const t=(now-t0)/1000;
  SC.forEach(([id,a,b])=>$('#'+id).classList.toggle('on',t>=a&&t<b));
  $('#s2').classList.toggle('clean',t>=8.5);
  $('#s6').classList.toggle('end',t>=23.9);
  $('#fade').classList.toggle('on',t>=24.4);
  ribT = (t>=17.5&&t<20.6)?1 : (t>=20.5&&t<23.9)?.35 : 0;
  draw(t);
  if(t<TOTAL)requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  else{running=false;$('#play').textContent='↻ Replay';$('#ui').style.display='flex'}
}
function start(){
  running=false;
  document.querySelectorAll('.scene').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('on','clean','end'));
  $('#fade').classList.remove('on');rib=0;ribT=0;last=0;
  $('#ui').style.display='none';
  requestAnimationFrame(n=>{t0=n;running=true;requestAnimationFrame(frame)});
}
$('#play').onclick=start;
addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.code==='Space'){e.preventDefault();start()}});
draw(0);
</script>
</body>
</html>

    Notes

    • Branding: the code contains no Instagram or Meta logos, no watermarks, and no claims like "100% safe" or "official". All on-screen text is exactly what you specified.
    • Your screenshots: I couldn't see them, so the phone UI is a recreation built from your list of settings. To use your real screenshots, replace the .settings block with an <img> of your screenshot. The switch animations would then need to be dropped or overlaid.
    • Switch behavior: switches turn ON to hide a section. "Stories" flips on and then back off, to show enable and disable.
    • Audio: none is included. A bass-swell intro, a soft whoosh on each feature, and a rising riser into the DOWNLOAD APK button would fit the timing.
    • Timing: scene times are in the SC array near the bottom of the script, so you can retime them easily.

    If you'd rather generate this with an AI video tool like Runway, Veo or Kling, I can write scene-by-scene prompts for it. Those tools usually garble UI text, so you would add the text and your real icon in an editor afterward.
